How totals were calculated

Monthly category size comes from the Summary sheet. Products, volumes, average prices and references come only from the Listings sheet. A line’s sales and units are the sum captured in the Top 100 listings from September 2025 through August 2026. They do not represent Mercado Livre’s full GMV. September 2026 was kept separate as a partial month.

Duplicate control

Listing, brand, seller and catalog rankings are independent samples and were never added together. Duplicate files for the same month were counted once. Nested subcategories under Mini Vehicles and Bicycles and Toy Vehicles were kept separate for analysis.

Scores from 1 to 5

Demand, growth and recurrence derive from the data. Importability, regulation, logistics, showroom fit, batteries and intellectual property combine title evidence with commercial assessment.

Recommended use

Use this report to build the showroom’s first curation. Before purchasing, validate NCM classification, taxes, certification, packaging, MOQ, technical support, intellectual property and landed cost in Brazil.
